PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
Specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village
and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience
the Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of
Ganganakote Village of Nyamathi Taluk of Davanagere District and to constitute a
new revenue subvillage called Tunga Badavane as specified in schedule.

Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Ganganakote village for conversion Tunga Badavane unrecorded habitation in to
hamlet/part of the said revenue village.
Now , therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said
Act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be
affected thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after
thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

(11649)

QOLBO BOF VE BW, Aocmawds 02, HAowo’, 90.928 won Ya
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date Specified above
will be considered by the state Government. Objection or suggestion may be
addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Davanagere District.

SCHEDULE

Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Tunga Badavane village of Nyamathi
Taluk of Davanagere District convert the same to form hamlet within the boundary of
Ganganakote Revenue village and same will herein after be named as called Tunga
Badavane of Ganganakote village in Nyamathi Taluk of Davanagere District.

PR-1642

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
No.:RC-CFS/309/2025 Karnataka Government Secretariat
E- 1942288 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, 560001
Date:13-11-2025

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose
s to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Bidarekere Village of Holalkere
Taluk of Chitradurga District and to constitute a new revenue villages called
Bidarekere A.K.Hatti as specified in schedule.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of of
Bidarekere village for conversion of Bidarekere A.K.Hatti unrecorded habitation in
to hamlet/part of the said revenue village.

Now , therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said
Act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to
be affected thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or
after thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official
Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the
date Specified above will be considered by the state Government.
Objection or suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy
Commissioner, Chitradurga District.
SCHEDULE

Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared ashabitation area of Bidarekere A.K.Hatti village of
Holalkere Taluk of Chitradurga District convert the same to form hamlet within the
boundary of Bidarekere Revenue village and same will herein after be named as
called Bidarekere A.K.Hatti of Bidarekere village in Holalkere Taluk of
Chitradurga District.

It is hereby declared that the extent of land indicated in the columns(4) above
with survey numbers specified in column (3) on which the said unrecorded habitation
located is proposed to be converted as habitation area(Gramatana) of Bidarekere
A.K.Hatti hamlet/part of Bidarekere village in Holalkere Taluk of Chitradurga
District.

The proposal specified above shall come into effect from such date as
the State Government may specify by Notification proposed to be issued under
section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 ( Karnataka Act No. 12 of
1964 )

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose
s to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Chikkasulikere Village of
Ramanagara Taluk of Bengaluru South District and to constitute a new revenue
villages called Kempaiahna Doddi as specified in schedule.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of of
Chikkasulikere village for conversion of Kempaiahna Doddi unrecorded habitation
in to hamlet/part of the said revenue village.

Now , therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said
Act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to
be affected thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or
afterthirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official
Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the
date Specified above will be considered by the state Government.
Objection or suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy
Commissioner, Bengaluru South District.
SCHEDULE

Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared ashabitation area of Kempaiahna Doddi village of
Ramanagara Taluk of Bengaluru South District convert the same to form hamlet
within the boundary of Chikkasulikere Revenue village and same will herein after be
named as called Kempaiahna Doddi of Chikkasulikere village in Ramanagara
Taluk of Bengaluru South District.

the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land

under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having

regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose s
to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Kamanahalli Village of

villages called Mavuraiahyanagudlu as specified in schedule.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose
s to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Yaradankalu Village of Tarikere
Taluk of Chikkamagaluru District and to constitute a new revenue villages called
Shree Narasimha Swamy Badavane as specified in schedule.

Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of of
Yaradankalu village for conversion of Shree Narasimha Swamy Badavane
unrecorded habitation in to hamlet/part of the said revenue village.

Now , therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said
Act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to
be affected thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or
after thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official
Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the
date Specified above will be considered by the state Government.
Objection or suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy
Commissioner, Chikkamagaluru District.

SCHEDULE

Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared ashabitation area of Shree Narasimha Swamy Badavane
village of Tarikere Taluk of Chikkamagaluru District convert the same to form
hamlet within the boundary of Yaradankalu Revenue village and same will herein
after be named as called Shree Narasimha Swamy Badavane of Yaradankalu
village in Tarikere Taluk of Chikkamagaluru District.

Date: 12-11-2025.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka
proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Pannapur Village of

Karatagi Taluk, Koppal District and to constitute a new revenue villages called
Janata Camp as specified in schedule along with maps with old survey numbers
and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Koppal District.

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village Pannapur Village of Karatagi Taluk, Koppal District to form a new revenue
village called as Janata Camp of Karatagi Taluk Koppal District along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose
s to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Hunasihala Village of Yelaburga
taluk Koppal District and to constitute a new revenue villages called Sri Rama
Nagar as specified in schedule.

Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Hunasihala village for conversion of Sri Rama Nagar unrecorded habitation in to
hamlet/ extension/ part of the said revenue village.

Now , therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said
Act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to
be affected thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or
after thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official
Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the
date Specified above will be considered by the state Government.
Objection or suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy
Commissioner, Koppal District.

table shall be declared ashabitation are aofcalled Sri Rama Nagar village of
Yelaburga Taluk of Koppal District convert the same to form hamlet/extension with
in the boundary of Hunasihala Revenue village and same will herein after be named
as called Sri Rama Nagar of Hunasihala villagein Yelaburga Taluk of Koppal
District.

REVISED PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue act, 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into considersation the
factors specified in clauses ( i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules
1966 such as the population , area under Land revenue and having regard to the
administrative convenience,the Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a
notification to alter the limits of Gundur Village of Hangal Taluk, Haveri district to
convert the existing Shivajipur into new revenue Village as specified in schedule.

A revenue village named Shivajipura from Gundura village in Hangal taluk
of Haveri district was declared as a revenue village by the government's final
notification number: RD 24 LRD 92 dated: 12-6-1995. Since the survey numbers were
not given in the said final notification in order, the previously issued final notification
has been cancelled and a revised preliminary notification has been issued.

Land bearing survey numbers specefiedin schedule –I shall be excluded from
the limits of Gundur village Hangal taluk Haveri district and the land bearing survey
numbers specified in schedule-II remain in the limits of Gundur village Hangal taluk
Haveri district after the formation of new revenue village called as Shivajipur of
Hangal taluk and Haveri district .
Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely tobe affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration after 30 days from the date of
publication of the notification in the official gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of the period
specified above will be considered by the state government. Objections or suggestion
may be addressed to the Deputy commissioner, Haveri District ,Haveri.

from the limits of Gundur villageof Hangal taluk Haveri district to form a new

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

E- 1858226 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru,560001
Date: 13-11-2025

REVISED PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the
factors specified in clause (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules
1966 such as the population , area under land revenue and having regard to the
administrative convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to alter the extent
of residential area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the
boundary of Hirur village of Hangal Taluk of Haveri District to convert the existing
Mahatma gandi badavane unrecorded habitation in to a hamlet/extention/part of the
existing revenue village. The area occupied by the said hamlet of the village is
specified in the schedule below.
The revenue village named Hanumanakoppa from Hirur village in Hanagal
taluk of Haveri district has been declared as a revenue village by the government's
final notification number: RD 11 L R D 95 dated: 25-07-1997, and since an
undocumented settlement cannot be designated as a village station, A notification is
hereby issued under Section 94-D of the Karnataka Land revenue Act, 1964 declaring
the land having the survey numbers and extent specified in the Schedule as a small
settlement/extent/part of the unregistered settlement Hanumanakoppa revenue
village.
Now therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 6 of the said
Act,notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration after thirty days from the
date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of period
specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Haveri distri.

SCHEDULE
Lands bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column (3) of the
table shall be declared as gramatana area of Hanumanakoppa of Hangal taluk of
Haveri district and convert the same to form hamlet extention within the boundary of
Hanumanakoppa Revenue village and same will here in after be named as
Hanumanakoppa of Hangal Taluk Haveri District.

PR - 1674
GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

No.:RC-CFS0ADMN/701/2025 Karnataka Government
Secretariat
E- 1909881 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, 560001
Date:12-11-2025.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land

Revenue Rules 1966such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose
s to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Sanvasagi Village of Hanagal Taluk
of Haveri District and to constitute a new revenue villages called Basavanna
Nagar as specified in schedule.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Sanvasagi village for conversion of Basavanna Nagar unrecorded habitation in to
hamlet/part of the said revenue village.

Now , therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said
Act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be
affected there by that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after
thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official
Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the
date Specified above will be considered by the state Government.
Objection or suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy
Commissioner, Haveri District.

SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Basavanna Nagar village of Hanagal
Taluk of Haveri District convert the same to form hamlet within the boundary of
Sanvasagi Revenue village and same will here in after be named as called Basavana
Nagar of Sanvasagi village in Hanagal Taluk of Haveri District.

Revenue Act,1964(Karnataka Act No 12 of 1964)and taking into consideration the
factors specified in clauses(i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules
1966 such as the population, area of the village,the demand under land revenue in the
village and other head of account and having regard to the administrative convenience,
the Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a notification to alter the limits of
Diksanga B village of Afzalpur taluk, Kalaburagi district and to constitute a new
village called Diksanga C as specified in schedule along with maps with old survey
numbers and new survey numbers assigned to them.
Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state government. Objections or suggestions may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Kalburgi District,

E- 1925008 Multi Storied Building

Date:12 -11 -2025

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the
factors specified in clauses (i) to (iv} of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules
1966 such as the population, area under land revenue and having regard to the
administrative convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to after, the extent
of residential area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the
boundary of Gobbur (b) Village of Afzalpur Taluk of Kalaburagi District to convert
the existing Gobbur (c) unrecorded habitation in to a hamlet/extension /part of the
existing revenue village. The area occupied by the said hamlet of the village is
specified in the schedule below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Gobbur (b) village for conversion of Gobbur (c) unrecorded habitation in to
hamlet/extension/ part of the said revenue village.

Now therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by Section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the person likely to be affected thereby
that the proposal will be taken into consideration after thirty days from the date of
publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of the period
specified above will be considered by the State Government Objections or suggestions
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner of Kalaburagi District.
SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column (3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Gobbur (c) village of AfzalpurTaluk of
Kalaburagi District convert the same to form hamlet/extension within the boundary
of Gobbur (b) Revenue village and same will herein after be named as Gobbur (c) of
Gobbur (b) village in Afzalpur Taluk of Kalaburagi District.

REVISED PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the
factors specified in clause (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules
1966 such as the population , area under land revenue and having regard to the
administrative convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to alter the extent
of residential area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the
boundary of Hirur village of Hangal Taluk of Haveri District to convert the existing
Mahatma gandi badavane unrecorded habitation in to a hamlet/extention/part of the
existing revenue village. The area occupied by the said hamlet of the village is
specified in the schedule below.
The revenue village named Hanumanakoppa from Hirur village in Hanagal
taluk of Haveri district has been declared as a revenue village by the government's
final notification number: RD 11 L R D 95 dated: 25-07-1997, and since an
undocumented settlement cannot be designated as a village station, A notification is
hereby issued under Section 94-D of the Karnataka Land revenue Act, 1964 declaring
the land having the survey numbers and extent specified in the Schedule as a small
settlement/extent/part of the unregistered settlement Hanumanakoppa revenue
village.
Now therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 6 of the said
Act,notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration after thirty days from the
date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of period
specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Haveri distri.

SCHEDULE

Lands bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column (3) of the
table shall be declared as gramatana area of Hanumanakoppa of Hangal taluk of
Haveri district and convert the same to form hamlet extention within the boundary of
Hanumanakoppa Revenue village and same will here in after be named as
Hanumanakoppa of Hangal Taluk Haveri District.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose
s to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Bislere Village of Kaduru Taluk of
Chikkamagaluru District and to constitute a new revenue villages called Bislere
Badavane -1 as specified in schedule.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of of
Bislere village for conversion of Bislere Badavane -1 unrecorded habitation in to
hamlet/part of the said revenue village.

Now , therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said
Act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to
be affected thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or
after thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official
Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the
date Specified above will be considered by the state Government.
Objection or suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy
Commissioner, Chikkamagaluru District.
SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared ashabitation area of Bislere Badavane -1 village of Kaduru
Taluk of Chikkamagaluru District convert the same to form hamlet within the
boundary of Bislere Revenue village and same will herein after be named as called
Bislere Badavane -1 of Bislere village in Kaduru Taluk of Chikkamagaluru
District.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the
factors specified in clauses (i) to (iv} of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules
1966 such as the population, area under land revenue and having regard to the
administrative convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to after, the extent
of residential area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the
boundary of Jhalaki (K) Village of Aland Taluk of Kalaburgi District to convert the
existing Padri Tanda unrecorded habitation in to a hamlet/extension /part of the
existing revenue village. The area occupied by the said hamlet of the village is
specified in the schedule below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Jhalaki (K) village for conversion of Padri Tanda unrecorded habitation in to
hamlet/extension/ part of the said revenue village.
Now therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by Section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the person likely to be affected thereby
that the proposal will be taken into consideration after thirty days from the date of
publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of the period
specified above will be considered by the State Government Objections or suggestions
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner of Kalaburgi District.
SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column (3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Padri Tanda village of Aland Taluk of
Kalaburgi District convert the same to form hamlet/extension within the boundary of
Jhalaki (K) Revenue village and same will here in after be named as Padri Tanda of
Jhalaki (K) village in Aland Taluk of Kalaburgi District.

No:PDS 74 SSM 2025 Karnataka Government Secretariat,

Bangalore, Dated: 05.11.2025 . .

NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by sub-section (1) of section 30 of the
Registration of Births and Deaths Act, 1969 (Central Act 18 of 1969) (Amended in
2023), the Government of Karnataka, hereby amend the Karnataka Registration of
Births and Deaths Rules, 1999 (Amended in 2024), with approval of the Central
Government hereby including additional column in Death Reporting Form, which
is prescribed in the Notification No.PDS 90 SMC 2024, dated.31.12.2024.

1. Title and Commencement. - (1) These rules may be called the Karnataka
Registration of Births and Deaths (Amendment) Rules, 2025.
(2) They shall come into force from the date of their publication in the official
Gazette.

2. The new column “EPIC No.of the deceased, if available (on voluntary
basis)” is included in the Statistical part of the Death reporting form (Form. 2). The
modified form is as under

By Order and in the Name of
Governor of Karnataka

(B.Dayananda)
Under Secretary to Government (S-5),
Planning, Programme monitoring and
Statistics Department.
